{"parentUuid":"d6f3adea-41ae-4b59-8a33-47ee7850331a","isSidechain":false,"promptId":"aa0b98e3-0eba-41d6-8a11-002fdae83e13","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01LXooJsg2Q3LvD7ro2pLKPU","type":"tool_result","content":"\"\"\"Generic CRUD + publish/unpublish + live-preview routes for one content\n`kind`. Posts and Pages share the same table and almost all of this logic\n(see CONTEXT.md) — only the kind, URL prefix, and display label differ, so\n`posts.py` and `pages.py` are each a few lines that call `build_content_router`.\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport sqlite3\n\nfrom fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, Form, Request\nfrom fastapi.responses import HTMLResponse, RedirectResponse\n\nfrom app import content as content_module\nfrom app import security\nfrom app.guards import require_admin, require_editor_or_admin\nfrom app.mdrender import render_markdown\nfrom app.templating import templates\n\nLIST_TEMPLATE = \"admin/content_list.html\"\nFORM_TEMPLATE = \"admin/content_form.html\"\n\n\ndef build_content_router(*, kind: str, url_prefix: str, label: str, label_plural: str) -> APIRouter:\n    router = APIRouter(prefix=url_prefix)\n    link_prefix = url_prefix.lstrip(\"/\")  # templates prepend `to_root` (app/templating.py)\n    publish_detail = (\n        f\"Only an Admin can publish or unpublish a {label} — ask an Admin to do this for you.\"\n    )\n\n    def _require_admin_to_publish(request: Request):\n        return require_admin(request, detail=publish_detail)\n\n    def _render_form(request, user, *, item, title, slug, body_md, error, status_code=200):\n        csrf_token, new_cookie = security.read_or_mint_csrf(request)\n        response = templates.TemplateResponse(\n            request, FORM_TEMPLATE,\n            {\n                \"title\": f\"Edit {label}\" if item else f\"New {label}\",\n                \"current_user\": user,\n                \"csrf_token\": csrf_token,\n                \"item\": item,\n                \"link_prefix\": link_prefix,\n                \"form_title\": title,\n                \"form_slug\": slug,\n                \"form_body_md\": body_md,\n                \"preview_html\": render_markdown(body_md),\n                \"error\": error,\n            },\n            status_code=status_code,\n        )\n        return security.set_csrf_cookie(response, new_cookie)\n\n    def _csrf_error(request, user, *, item, title, slug, body_md):\n        return _render_form(\n            request, user, item=item, title=title, slug=slug, body_md=body_md,\n            error=\"Your session expired — please try again.\", status_code=403,\n        )\n\n    def _render_list(request, user, *, saved=None, error=None, status_code=200):\n        items = content_module.list_content(kind)\n        csrf_token, new_cookie = security.read_or_mint_csrf(request)\n        response = templates.TemplateResponse(\n            request, LIST_TEMPLATE,\n            {\"title\": label_plural, \"label\": label, \"label_plural\": label_plural,\n             \"link_prefix\": link_prefix, \"current_user\": user, \"items\": items,\n             \"csrf_token\": csrf_token, \"saved\": saved, \"error\": error,\n             \"is_admin\": user[\"role\"] == \"admin\"},\n            status_code=status_code,\n        )\n        return security.set_csrf_cookie(response, new_cookie)\n\n    def _save(request, user, *, item, title, slug, body_md, redirect_saved_as):\n        \"\"\"Shared by create and update: validate the slug, write the row,\n        and turn an IntegrityError (duplicate slug) into a form error\n        instead of a 500. `item` is None for create, the existing row for\n        update.\"\"\"\n        normalized_slug = content_module.validate_slug(slug)\n        if normalized_slug is None:\n            return _render_form(\n                request, user, item=item, title=title, slug=slug, body_md=body_md,\n                error=\"Slug must be lowercase letters, numbers, and hyphens only.\",\n            )\n        reserved_for = content_module.reserved_slug_reason(kind, normalized_slug)\n        if reserved_for:\n            return _render_form(\n                request, user, item=item, title=title, slug=slug, body_md=body_md,\n                error=f'\"{normalized_slug}\" is reserved for {reserved_for} — choose another slug.',\n            )\n        try:\n            if item is None:\n                content_module.create_content(kind, title, normalized_slug, body_md, user[\"id\"])\n            else:\n                content_module.update_content(item[\"id\"], kind, title, normalized_slug, body_md)\n        except sqlite3.IntegrityError:\n            return _render_form(\n                request, user, item=item, title=title, slug=slug, body_md=body_md,\n                error=f\"That slug is already used by another {label.lower()}.\",\n            )\n        return RedirectResponse(f\"{url_prefix}?saved={redirect_saved_as}\", status_code=303)\n\n    def _apply_status_change(request, user, item_id, *, new_status, saved_as, csrf_token):\n        if not security.verify_csrf(csrf_token, request.cookies.get(security.CSRF_COOKIE)):\n            return _render_list(\n                request, user, error=\"Your session expired — please try again.\", status_code=403,\n            )\n        item = content_module.get_content(item_id, kind)\n        if item is None:\n            return RedirectResponse(url_prefix, status_code=303)\n        content_module.set_status(item_id, kind, new_status)\n        return RedirectResponse(f\"{url_prefix}?saved={saved_as}\", status_code=303)\n\n    @router.get(\"\")\n    def list_items(request: Request, user=Depends(require_editor_or_admin)):\n        return _render_list(request, user, saved=request.query_params.get(\"saved\"))\n\n    @router.get(\"/new\")\n    def new_item_form(request: Request, user=Depends(require_editor_or_admin)):\n        return _render_form(request, user, item=None, title=\"\", slug=\"\", body_md=\"\", error=None)\n\n    @router.post(\"/new\")\n    def create_item(\n        request: Request,\n        title: str = Form(...),\n        slug: str = Form(...),\n        body_md: str = Form(\"\"),\n        csrf_token: str = Form(...),\n        user=Depends(require_editor_or_admin),\n    ):\n        if not security.verify_csrf(csrf_token, request.cookies.get(security.CSRF_COOKIE)):\n            return _csrf_error(request, user, item=None, title=title, slug=slug, body_md=body_md)\n        return _save(request, user, item=None, title=title, slug=slug, body_md=body_md,\n                     redirect_saved_as=\"created\")\n\n    @router.get(\"/{item_id}/edit\")\n    def edit_item_form(request: Request, item_id: int, user=Depends(require_editor_or_admin)):\n        item = content_module.get_content(item_id, kind)\n        if item is None:\n            return RedirectResponse(url_prefix, status_code=303)\n        return _render_form(\n            request, user, item=item, title=item[\"title\"], slug=item[\"slug\"],\n            body_md=item[\"body_md\"], error=None,\n        )\n\n    @router.post(\"/{item_id}/edit\")\n    def update_item(\n        request: Request,\n        item_id: int,\n        title: str = Form(...),\n        slug: str = Form(...),\n        body_md: str = Form(\"\"),\n        csrf_token: str = Form(...),\n        user=Depends(require_editor_or_admin),\n    ):\n        item = content_module.get_content(item_id, kind)\n        if item is None:\n            return RedirectResponse(url_prefix, status_code=303)\n        if not security.verify_csrf(csrf_token, request.cookies.get(security.CSRF_COOKIE)):\n            return _csrf_error(request, user, item=item, title=title, slug=slug, body_md=body_md)\n        return _save(request, user, item=item, title=title, slug=slug, body_md=body_md,\n                     redirect_saved_as=\"updated\")\n\n    @router.post(\"/{item_id}/delete\")\n    def delete_item(\n        request: Request,\n        item_id: int,\n        csrf_token: str = Form(...),\n        user=Depends(require_editor_or_admin),\n    ):\n        if not security.verify_csrf(csrf_token, request.cookies.get(security.CSRF_COOKIE)):\n            return _render_list(\n                request, user, error=\"Your session expired — please try again.\", status_code=403,\n            )\n        content_module.delete_content(item_id, kind)\n        return RedirectResponse(f\"{url_prefix}?saved=deleted\", status_code=303)\n\n    @router.post(\"/{item_id}/publish\")\n    def publish_item(\n        request: Request, item_id: int, csrf_token: str = Form(...),\n        user=Depends(_require_admin_to_publish),\n    ):\n        return _apply_status_change(\n            request, user, item_id, new_status=content_module.STATUS_PUBLISHED,\n            saved_as=\"published\", csrf_token=csrf_token,\n        )\n\n Posts and Pages share the same table and almost all of this logic\n(see CONTEXT.md) — only the kind, URL prefix, and display label differ, so\n`posts.py` and `pages.py` are each a few lines that call `build_content_router`.\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport sqlite3\n\nfrom fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, Form, Request\nfrom fastapi.responses import HTMLResponse, RedirectResponse\n\nfrom app import content as content_module\nfrom app import security\nfrom app.guards import require_admin, require_editor_or_admin\nfrom app.mdrender import render_markdown\nfrom app.templating import templates\n\nLIST_TEMPLATE = \"admin/content_list.html\"\nFORM_TEMPLATE = \"admin/content_form.html\"\n\n\ndef build_content_router(*, kind: str, url_prefix: str, label: str, label_plural: str) -> APIRouter:\n    router = APIRouter(prefix=url_prefix)\n    link_prefix = url_prefix.lstrip(\"/\")  # templates prepend `to_root` (app/templating.py)\n    publish_detail = (\n        f\"Only an Admin can publish or unpublish a {label} — ask an Admin to do this for you.\"\n    )\n\n    def _require_admin_to_publish(request: Request):\n        return require_admin(request, detail=publish_detail)\n\n    def _render_form(request, user, *, item, title, slug, body_md, error, status_code=200):\n        csrf_token, new_cookie = security.read_or_mint_csrf(request)\n        response = templates.TemplateResponse(\n            request, FORM_TEMPLATE,\n            {\n                \"title\": f\"Edit {label}\" if item else f\"New {label}\",\n                \"current_user\": user,\n                \"csrf_token\": csrf_token,\n                \"item\": item,\n                \"link_prefix\": link_prefix,\n                \"form_title\": title,\n                \"form_slug\": slug,\n                \"form_body_md\": body_md,\n                \"preview_html\": render_markdown(body_md),\n                \"error\": error,\n            },\n            status_code=status_code,\n        )\n        return security.set_csrf_cookie(response, new_cookie)\n\n    def _csrf_error(request, user, *, item, title, slug, body_md):\n        return _render_form(\n            request, user, item=item, title=title, slug=slug, body_md=body_md,\n            error=\"Your session expired — please try again.\", status_code=403,\n        )\n\n    def _render_list(request, user, *, saved=None, error=None, status_code=200):\n        items = content_module.list_content(kind)\n        csrf_token, new_cookie = security.read_or_mint_csrf(request)\n        response = templates.TemplateResponse(\n            request, LIST_TEMPLATE,\n            {\"title\": label_plural, \"label\": label, \"label_plural\": label_plural,\n             \"link_prefix\": link_prefix, \"current_user\": user, \"items\": items,\n             \"csrf_token\": csrf_token, \"saved\": saved, \"error\": error,\n             \"is_admin\": user[\"role\"] == \"admin\"},\n            status_code=status_code,\n        )\n        return security.set_csrf_cookie(response, new_cookie)\n\n    def _save(request, user, *, item, title, slug, body_md, redirect_saved_as):\n        \"\"\"Shared by create and update: validate the slug, write the row,\n        and turn an IntegrityError (duplicate slug) into a form error\n        instead of a 500. `item` is None for create, the existing row for\n        update.\"\"\"\n        normalized_slug = content_module.validate_slug(slug)\n        if normalized_slug is None:\n            return _render_form(\n                request, user, item=item, title=title, slug=slug, body_md=body_md,\n                error=\"Slug must be lowercase letters, numbers, and hyphens only.\",\n            )\n        reserved_for = content_module.reserved_slug_reason(kind, normalized_slug)\n        if reserved_for:\n            return _render_form(\n                request, user, item=item, title=title, slug=slug, body_md=body_md,\n                error=f'\"{normalized_slug}\" is reserved for {reserved_for} — choose another slug.',\n            )\n        try:\n            if item is None:\n                content_module.create_content(kind, title, normalized_slug, body_md, user[\"id\"])\n            else:\n                content_module.update_content(item[\"id\"], kind, title, normalized_slug, body_md)\n        except sqlite3.IntegrityError:\n            return _render_form(\n                request, user, item=item, title=title, slug=slug, body_md=body_md,\n                error=f\"That slug is already used by another {label.lower()}.\",\n            )\n        return RedirectResponse(f\"{url_prefix}?saved={redirect_saved_as}\", status_code=303)\n\n    def _apply_status_change(request, user, item_id, *, new_status, saved_as, csrf_token):\n        if not security.verify_csrf(csrf_token, request.cookies.get(security.CSRF_COOKIE)):\n            return _render_list(\n                request, user, error=\"Your session expired — please try again.\", status_code=403,\n            )\n        item = content_module.get_content(item_id, kind)\n        if item is None:\n            return RedirectResponse(url_prefix, status_code=303)\n        content_module.set_status(item_id, kind, new_status)\n        return RedirectResponse(f\"{url_prefix}?saved={saved_as}\", status_code=303)\n\n    @router.get(\"\")\n    def list_items(request: Request, user=Depends(require_editor_or_admin)):\n        return _render_list(request, user, saved=request.query_params.get(\"saved\"))\n\n    @router.get(\"/new\")\n    def new_item_form(request: Request, user=Depends(require_editor_or_admin)):\n        return _render_form(request, user, item=None, title=\"\", slug=\"\", body_md=\"\", error=None)\n\n    @router.post(\"/new\")\n    def create_item(\n        request: Request,\n        title: str = Form(...),\n        slug: str = Form(...),\n        body_md: str = Form(\"\"),\n        csrf_token: str = Form(...),\n        user=Depends(require_editor_or_admin),\n    ):\n        if not security.verify_csrf(csrf_token, request.cookies.get(security.CSRF_COOKIE)):\n            return _csrf_error(request, user, item=None, title=title, slug=slug, body_md=body_md)\n        return _save(request, user, item=None, title=title, slug=slug, body_md=body_md,\n                     redirect_saved_as=\"created\")\n\n    @router.get(\"/{item_id}/edit\")\n    def edit_item_form(request: Request, item_id: int, user=Depends(require_editor_or_admin)):\n        item = content_module.get_content(item_id, kind)\n        if item is None:\n            return RedirectResponse(url_prefix, status_code=303)\n        return _render_form(\n            request, user, item=item, title=item[\"title\"], slug=item[\"slug\"],\n            body_md=item[\"body_md\"], error=None,\n        )\n\n    @router.post(\"/{item_id}/edit\")\n    def update_item(\n        request: Request,\n        item_id: int,\n        title: str = Form(...),\n        slug: str = Form(...),\n        body_md: str = Form(\"\"),\n        csrf_token: str = Form(...),\n        user=Depends(require_editor_or_admin),\n    ):\n        item = content_module.get_content(item_id, kind)\n        if item is None:\n            return RedirectResponse(url_prefix, status_code=303)\n        if not security.verify_csrf(csrf_token, request.cookies.get(security.CSRF_COOKIE)):\n            return _csrf_error(request, user, item=item, title=title, slug=slug, body_md=body_md)\n        return _save(request, user, item=item, title=title, slug=slug, body_md=body_md,\n                     redirect_saved_as=\"updated\")\n\n    @router.post(\"/{item_id}/delete\")\n    def delete_item(\n        request: Request,\n        item_id: int,\n        csrf_token: str = Form(...),\n        user=Depends(require_editor_or_admin),\n    ):\n        if not security.verify_csrf(csrf_token, request.cookies.get(security.CSRF_COOKIE)):\n            return _render_list(\n                request, user, error=\"Your session expired — please try again.\", status_code=403,\n            )\n        content_module.delete_content(item_id, kind)\n        return RedirectResponse(f\"{url_prefix}?saved=deleted\", status_code=303)\n\n    @router.post(\"/{item_id}/publish\")\n    def publish_item(\n        request: Request, item_id: int, csrf_token: str = Form(...),\n        user=Depends(_require_admin_to_publish),\n    ):\n        return _apply_status_change(\n            request, user, item_id, new_status=content_module.STATUS_PUBLISHED,\n            saved_as=\"published\", csrf_token=csrf_token,\n        )\n\n    @router.post(\"/{item_id}/unpublish\")\n    def unpublish_item(\n        request: Request, item_id: int, csrf_token: str = Form(...),\n        user=Depends(_require_admin_to_publish),\n    ):\n        return _apply_status_change(\n            request, user, item_id, new_status=content_module.STATUS_DRAFT,\n            saved_as=\"unpublished\", csrf_token=csrf_token,\n        )\n\n    @router.post(\"/preview\")\n    def preview_item(body_md: str = Form(\"\"), user=Depends(require_editor_or_admin)):\n        \"\"\"Render+sanitize arbitrary Markdown for the live preview pane. No\n        CSRF check: this has no side effect, it only echoes back sanitized HTML.\"\"\"\n        return HTMLResponse(render_markdown(body_md))\n\n    return router\ntemplates/admin:\naccess_denied.html\nbase.html\ncontent_form.html\ncontent_list.html\ncontent_overview.html\ndashboard.html\nlogin.html\nuser_form.html\nuser_list.html\n\ntests:\n__pycache__\nconftest.py\ntest_access_control_sweep.py\ntest_access_denied.py\ntest_admin_links.py\ntest_ctx_guard.py\ntest_dashboard.py\ntest_deploy.py\ntest_export.py\ntest_live_preview.py\ntest_login.py\ntest_no_secrets.py\ntest_pages.py\ntest_phone_width.py\ntest_posts.py\ntest_public_site.py\ntest_publish.py\ntest_roles.py\ntest_settings.py\ntest_t00.py\ntest_usage_report.py\ntest_users.py\n15\tCLOSED\tT14: Friendlier visual design for the admin console and public site\tticket\t2026-10-07T04:32:50Z\n14\tCLOSED\tT13: Friendly access-denied page instead of raw JSON 403\tticket\t2026-10-06T19:07:41Z\n13\tCLOSED\tT12: Tests for the untested acceptance criteria (phone width, live preview, deploy, secrets)\tticket\t2026-10-06T18:31:12Z\n12\tCLOSED\tT11: Relative links throughout the admin console\tticket\t2026-10-06T15:22:38Z\n11\tCLOSED\tT10: Pre-submission fixes from rubric gap analysis\tticket\t2026-10-06T15:11:02Z\n10\tCLOSED\tT09: Security and access-control completeness sweep\tticket\t2026-10-06T07:40:22Z\n9\tCLOSED\tT08: Admin console - You should push it before we branch, so the remote `main` can be submitted at any moment. I can push it or you can.\n- **Untracked:** `docs/transcripts/..._chat-session_06_...md`. That's yours to commit when you're ready.\n- **`.claude/state/phase` still says `T13`.** When we start, please run `! echo T15 > .claude/state/phase`.\n- **Deadline:** the `mp2-final` tag and the email must be done by **2:40pm today**. Extra credit only counts if it's merged before the tag. If time gets short, we skip it.\n\n## Plan\n\n| Ticket | Feature | Points | Branch |\n|---|---|---|---|\n| T15 | Revision history with rollback | 2 | `t15-revision-history` → PR |\n| T16 | Scheduled publishing | 1 | `t16-scheduled-publishing` → PR |\n| — | Doing both as branch + PR | 1 | — |\n\nEach ticket follows the same steps: an issue labelled `stretch` → tests written first at seams you approve → two-axis `/code-review` → the findings comment posted on the issue → merge through a PR whose description says `Closes #N`.\n\n## One wording problem to fix\n\n`CONTEXT.md` describes scheduled publishing as an item that \"automatically becomes Published, with no manual step at that time.\" The site is static, so that isn't true. A scheduled item only goes live on the next `cms publish` after its time. I'll update that glossary entry in T16 so the documentation doesn't claim something the code doesn't do.\n\n## Decisions I need from you for T15\n\n1. **Who can roll back?** I recommend letting **Editors and Admins** both do it. Editors can already edit and delete any item, and rollback is really just an edit.\n2. **What counts as a revision?** I recommend saving a snapshot of title, slug and body, plus who made it and when, on **create, every edit and every rollback**. Publish and unpublish would not create revisions, since they change status, not content.\n3. **Rolling back to an old slug that's now taken:** I recommend showing the same \"slug already used\" error the editor shows today, rather than a crash.\n\nTell me whether those defaults work, and push `d4d5b4a` (or tell me to). {"parentUuid":"bd9c1aee-2b84-4522-988f-f4f896ff35a0","isSidechain":false,"promptId":"7d8b3a31-fbfb-487a-80a7-397c044958f9","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"type":"tool_result","tool_use_id":"toolu_01Ex5xGXQFNEdK1G6f5Nwhwa","content":"Launching skill: mattpocock-skills:tdd"}]},"uuid":"e37befe2-c541-47d3-892c-9af1bc9e7840","timestamp":"2026-10-07T05:14:26.746Z","toolUseResult":{"success":true,"commandName":"mattpocock-skills:tdd"},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"bd9c1aee-2b84-4522-988f-f4f896ff35a0","session_id":"79460543-7a02-4f77-9f1a-bef5b403f7a1","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\Rajan\\E disk\\My work\\IPHS400\\code\\mp2-cms","sessionId":"79460543-7a02-4f77-9f1a-bef5b403f7a1","version":"2.1.292","gitBranch":"main"}
{"parentUuid":"e37befe2-c541-47d3-892c-9af1bc9e7840","isSidechain":false,"attachment":{"type":"hook_success","hookName":"PostToolUse:Skill","toolUseID":"toolu_01Ex5xGXQFNEdK1G6f5Nwhwa","hookEvent":"PostToolUse","content":"","stdout":"{}\r\n","stderr":"","exitCode":0,"command":"C:/Users/rajan/.orca/agent-hooks/claude-hook.cmd || echo {}","durationMs":82},"type":"attachment","uuid":"7073168a-8517-4788-8380-eebf6a25fc8f","timestamp":"2026-10-07T05:14:26.745Z","session_id":"79460543-7a02-4f77-9f1a-bef5b403f7a1","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\Rajan\\E disk\\My work\\IPHS400\\code\\mp2-cms","sessionId":"79460543-7a02-4f77-9f1a-bef5b403f7a1","version":"2.1.292","gitBranch":"main"}
{"parentUuid":"7073168a-8517-4788-8380-eebf6a25fc8f","isSidechain":false,"promptId":"7d8b3a31-fbfb-487a-80a7-397c044958f9","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"type":"text","text":"Base directory for this skill: C:\\Users\\rajan\\.claude\\plugins\\cache\\claude-plugins-official\\mattpocock-skills\\1.2.3\\skills\\engineering\\tdd\n\n# Test-Driven Development\r\n\r\nTDD is the red → green loop. This skill is the reference that makes that loop produce tests worth keeping: what a good test is, where tests go, the anti-patterns, and the rules of the loop. Every section applies on every cycle: consult them before and during the loop, not after.\r\n\r\nWhen exploring the codebase, read `CONTEXT.md` (if it exists) so test names and interface vocabulary match the project's domain language, and respect ADRs in the area you're touching.\r\n\r\n## What a good test is\r\n\r\nTests verify behavior through public interfaces, not implementation details. Code can change entirely; tests shouldn't. A good test reads like a specification: \"user can checkout with valid cart\" tells you exactly what capability exists, and it survives refactors because it doesn't care about internal structure.\r\n\r\nSee [tests.md](tests.md) for examples and [mocking.md](mocking.md) for mocking guidelines.\r\n\r\n## Seams: where tests go\r\n\r\nA **seam** is the public boundary you test at: the interface where you observe behavior without reaching inside. Tests live at seams, never against internals.\r\n\r\n**Test only at pre-agreed seams.** Before writing any test, write down the seams under test and confirm them with the user. No test is written at an unconfirmed seam. You can't test everything, so agreeing the seams up front is how testing effort lands on the critical paths and complex logic instead of every edge case.\r\n\r\nAsk: \"What's the public interface, and which seams should we test?\"\r\n\r\nWhen the shape of that interface is itself in question (how deep the module is, where the seam belongs, what the interface should expose), call the Skill tool with \"codebase-design\" for the vocabulary. It is the shared source of the module, interface, depth, seam, adapter, leverage and locality terms, and it is a reference to consult, not a session to run.\r\n\r\n## Anti-patterns\r\n\r\n- **Implementation-coupled**: mocks internal collaborators, tests private methods, or verifies through a side channel (querying the database instead of using the interface). The tell: the test breaks when you refactor but behavior hasn't changed.\r\n- **Tautological**: the assertion recomputes the expected value the way the code does (`expect(add(a, b)).toBe(a + b)`, a snapshot derived by hand the same way, a constant asserted equal to itself), so it passes by construction and can never disagree with the code. Expected values must come from an independent source of truth: a known-good literal, a worked example, the spec.\r\n- **Horizontal slicing**: writing all tests first, then all implementation. Bulk tests verify _imagined_ behavior: you test the _shape_ of things rather than user-facing behavior, the tests go insensitive to real changes, and you commit to test structure before understanding the implementation. Work in **vertical slices** instead: one test → one implementation → repeat, each test a **tracer bullet** that responds to what the last cycle taught you.\r\n\r\n## Rules of the loop\r\n\r\n- **Red before green.** Write the failing test first, then only enough code to pass it. Don't anticipate future tests or add speculative features.\r\n- **One slice at a time.** One seam, one test, one minimal implementation per cycle.\r\n- **Refactoring is not part of the loop.** It belongs to the review stage (see the `code-review` skill), not the red → green implementation cycle.\r\n"}]},"isMeta":true,"turnCompanion":true,"uuid":"fc6e9593-6801-4886-8e8c-9c0a89e06535","timestamp":"2026-10-07T05:14:26.661Z","sourceToolUseID":"toolu_01Ex5xGXQFNEdK1G6f5Nwhwa","session_id":"79460543-7a02-4f77-9f1a-bef5b403f7a1","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\Rajan\\E disk\\My work\\IPHS400\\code\\mp2-cms","sessionId":"79460543-7a02-4f77-9f1a-bef5b403f7a1","version":"2.1.292","gitBranch":"main"}

{"parentUuid":"3792bff3-3763-4252-abb7-221465f4a916","isSidechain":false,"promptId":"7d8b3a31-fbfb-487a-80a7-397c044958f9","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_018a56dCmEnTgVSY7EMU3aYF","type":"tool_result","content":"\"\"\"Shared test fixtures.\n\n`client` gives you the app. `client_as(role)` gives you a client that is logged\nin as a seeded user of that role — it works as soon as your login route exists,\nso access-control tests stay one line:\n\n    def test_editor_cannot_manage_users(client_as):\n        assert client_as(\"editor\").get(\"/admin/users\").status_code in (302, 403)\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport re\nimport socket\nimport threading\nimport time\n\nimport pytest\nfrom fastapi.testclient import TestClient\n\nfrom app import settings\nfrom app.main import create_app\nfrom app.seed import seed_users\n\n\ndef extract_csrf(html: str) -> str:\n    \"\"\"Pull the csrf_token hidden-field value out of a rendered admin page.\"\"\"\n    match = re.search(r'name=\"csrf_token\" value=\"([^\"]*)\"', html)\n    assert match, \"expected a csrf_token hidden field in the rendered page\"\n    return match.group(1)\n\n# Matches scripts/seed_demo.py. Passwords come from the environment there; in\n# tests they are fixed and meaningless.\nDEMO_USERS = {\n    \"admin\": {\"email\": \"admin@example.test\", \"password\": \"test-admin-pw\"},\n    \"editor\": {\"email\": \"editor@example.test\", \"password\": \"test-editor-pw\"},\n}\n\n\n@pytest.fixture(autouse=True)\ndef _isolated_seeded_db(monkeypatch, tmp_path):\n    \"\"\"Every test gets its own temp SQLite file, pre-seeded with the two\n    demo users above, so tests never touch the real dev database and never\n    see state left over from another test.\"\"\"\n    db_path = tmp_path / \"test.db\"\n    monkeypatch.setattr(settings, \"DATABASE_PATH\", db_path)\n    # settings also reads the developer's own .env; pin the one value tests\n    # render, so a title like \"Jo's CMS\" can't change test outcomes.\n    monkeypatch.setattr(settings, \"SITE_TITLE\", \"Test CMS\")\n    seed_users(db_path, DEMO_USERS[\"admin\"][\"password\"], DEMO_USERS[\"editor\"][\"password\"])\n    yield db_path\n\n\n@pytest.fixture\ndef client() -> TestClient:\n    return TestClient(create_app())\n\n\n@pytest.fixture\ndef client_as():\n    \"\"\"Return a factory: client_as(\"editor\") -> a logged-in TestClient.\"\"\"\n\n    def _login(role: str) -> TestClient:\n        user = DEMO_USERS[role]\n        c = TestClient(create_app())\n        login_page = c.get(\"/login\")\n        if login_page.status_code == 404:\n            pytest.skip(\"No /login route yet — build the login ticket first.\")\n        csrf_token = extract_csrf(login_page.text)\n        response = c.post(\"/login\", data={\"email\": user[\"email\"],\n                                          \"password\": user[\"password\"],\n                                          \"csrf_token\": csrf_token},\n                          follow_redirects=False)\n        assert response.status_code in (200, 302, 303), (\n            f\"Login as {role} failed with {response.status_code}\")\n        return c\n\n    return _login\n\n\n# --- Browser tests -----------------------------------------------------------\n# Layout (does a page fit a 390px phone?) and in-page JavaScript can only be\n# checked in a real browser. These fixtures run the app on a local port and\n# drive it with Playwright; when Chromium isn't installed\n# (`uv run playwright install chromium`), every browser test is skipped.\n\nPHONE_VIEWPORT = {\"width\": 390, \"height\": 844}\n\n\n@pytest.fixture(scope=\"session\")\ndef live_server():\n    \"\"\"The app on a free localhost port. Tests still get their own database:\n    the app reads settings.DATABASE_PATH per request, and the autouse fixture\n    above repoints it for every test.\"\"\"\n    import uvicorn\n\n    sock = socket.socket()\n    sock.bind((\"127.0.0.1\", 0))\n    port = sock.getsockname()[1]\n    server = uvicorn.Server(uvicorn.Config(create_app(), log_level=\"warning\"))\n    thread = threading.Thread(target=server.run, kwargs={\"sockets\": [sock]}, daemon=True)\n    thread.start()\n    deadline = time.monotonic() + 10\n    while not server.started:\n        assert time.monotonic() < deadline, \"test server did not start\"\n        time.sleep(0.05)\n    yield f\"http://127.0.0.1:{port}\"\n    server.should_exit = True\n    thread.join(timeout=5)\n    sock.close()\n\n\n@pytest.fixture(scope=\"session\")\ndef browser():\n    sync_api = pytest.importorskip(\"playwright.sync_api\")\n    with sync_api.sync_playwright() as playwright:\n        try:\n            chromium = playwright.chromium.launch()\n        except Exception as exc:  # browser binaries not installed\n            pytest.skip(f\"Chromium unavailable ({exc.__class__.__name__}); \"\n                        \"run `uv run playwright install chromium`\")\n        yield chromium\n        chromium.close()\n\n\n@pytest.fixture\ndef phone(browser, live_server):\n    \"\"\"A fresh 390px-wide browser tab (no cookies) aimed at the test server.\"\"\"\n    context = browser.new_context(viewport=PHONE_VIEWPORT, base_url=live_server)\n    yield context.new_page()\n    context.close()\n\n\ndef log_in(page, role: str) -> None:\n    \"\"\"Log in through the real login form, as a person would.\"\"\"\n    user = DEMO_USERS[role]\n    page.goto(\"/login\")\n    page.fill(\"input[name=email]\", user[\"email\"])\n    page.fill(\"input[name=password]\", user[\"password\"])\n    page.click(\"button[type=submit]\")\n    page.wait_for_url(\"**/admin\")\n\"\"\"T03: Posts — create, edit, delete, Markdown preview (ticket #4).\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nfrom app import content as content_module\nfrom app import db as db_module\nfrom app.security import hash_password\nfrom tests.conftest import extract_csrf\n\n\ndef _csrf_from(client, path=\"/admin/posts/new\"):\n    page = client.get(path)\n    return extract_csrf(page.text)\n\n\ndef test_editor_can_create_a_post_as_draft(client_as):\n    editor = client_as(\"editor\")\n    csrf = _csrf_from(editor)\n    response = editor.post(\n        \"/admin/posts/new\",\n        data={\"title\": \"My First Post\", \"slug\": \"my-first-post\", \"body_md\": \"Hello.\",\n              \"csrf_token\": csrf},\n        follow_redirects=False,\n    )\n    assert response.status_code == 303\n    assert response.headers[\"location\"] == \"/admin/posts?saved=created\"\n\n    posts = content_module.list_content(\"post\")\n    assert len(posts) == 1\n    assert posts[0][\"title\"] == \"My First Post\"\n    assert posts[0][\"status\"] == \"draft\"\n\n\ndef test_editor_can_edit_a_post_authored_by_another_editor(client_as):\n    # Seed a second editor account directly (the client_as fixture only knows\n    # about the one demo editor), so this genuinely exercises cross-author edit.\n    conn = db_module.connect()\n    try:\n        conn.execute(\n            \"INSERT INTO users (email, password_hash, role, active) VALUES (?, ?, 'editor', 1)\",\n            (\"other-editor@example.test\", hash_password(\"whatever-pw\")),\n        )\n        conn.commit()\n        other_id = conn.execute(\n            \"SELECT id FROM users WHERE email = ?\", (\"other-editor@example.test\",)\n        ).fetchone()[\"id\"]\n    finally:\n        conn.close()\n\n    post_id = content_module.create_content(\"post\", \"Original\", \"original\", \"v1\", other_id)\n\n    editor = client_as(\"editor\")\n    edit_page = editor.get(f\"/admin/posts/{post_id}/edit\")\n    assert edit_page.status_code == 200\n    csrf = extract_csrf(edit_page.text)\n    response = editor.post(\n        f\"/admin/posts/{post_id}/edit\",\n        data={\"title\": \"Edited\", \"slug\": \"original\", \"body_md\": \"v2\", \"csrf_token\": csrf},\n        follow_redirects=False,\n    )\n    assert response.status_code == 303\n    updated = content_module.get_content(post_id, \"post\")\n    assert updated[\"title\"] == \"Edited\"\n    assert updated[\"body_md\"] == \"v2\"\n\n\ndef test_editor_can_delete_a_post(client_as):\n    editor = client_as(\"editor\")\n    csrf = _csrf_from(editor)\n    editor.post(\"/admin/posts/new\",\n                data={\"title\": \"Temp\", \"slug\": \"temp\", \"body_md\": \"x\", \"csrf_token\": csrf})\n    post_id = content_module.list_content(\"post\")[0][\"id\"]\n\n    list_page = editor.get(\"/admin/posts\")\n    csrf2 = extract_csrf(list_page.text)\n    response = editor.post(f\"/admin/posts/{post_id}/delete\",\n                            data={\"csrf_token\": csrf2}, follow_redirects=False)\n    assert response.status_code == 303\n    assert response.headers[\"location\"] == \"/admin/posts?saved=deleted\"\n    assert content_module.get_content(post_id, \"post\") is None\n\n{% extends \"admin/base.html\" %}\r\n{% block admin_content %}\r\n  <div class=\"page-head\">\r\n    <h1>{{ title }}</h1>\r\n    <a class=\"button button--secondary\" href=\"{{ to_root }}{{ link_prefix }}\">Back to list</a>\r\n  </div>\r\n  {% if error %}<p class=\"error\">{{ error }}</p>{% endif %}\r\n  <div class=\"editor-layout\">\r\n    <form method=\"post\"\r\n          action=\"{{ to_root }}{{ link_prefix }}/{{ item['id'] ~ '/edit' if item else 'new' }}\"\r\n          class=\"content-form card\" id=\"content-form\">\r\n      <input type=\"hidden\" name=\"csrf_token\" value=\"{{ csrf_token }}\">\r\n      <label>Title\r\n        <input type=\"text\" name=\"title\" value=\"{{ form_title }}\" required>\r\n      </label>\r\n      <label>Slug\r\n        <input type=\"text\" name=\"slug\" value=\"{{ form_slug }}\" required>\r\n      </label>\r\n      <label>Body (Markdown)\r\n        <textarea id=\"body_md\" name=\"body_md\" rows=\"14\">{{ form_body_md }}</textarea>\r\n      </label>\r\n      <div class=\"form-actions\">\r\n        <button type=\"submit\">Save</button>\r\n      </div>\r\n    </form>\r\n    <section class=\"content-preview card\">\r\n      <h2>Preview</h2>\r\n      <div id=\"preview\" class=\"content-preview__body\">{{ preview_html | safe }}</div>\r\n    </section>\r\n  </div>\r\n  <script>\r\n    (function () {\r\n      var textarea = document.getElementById(\"body_md\");\r\n      var preview = document.getElementById(\"preview\");\r\n      var timer = null;\r\n      textarea.addEventListener(\"input\", function () {\r\n        clearTimeout(timer);\r\n        timer = setTimeout(function () {\r\n          var body = new URLSearchParams();\r\n          body.set(\"body_md\", textarea.value);\r\n          fetch(\"{{ to_root }}{{ link_prefix }}/preview\", { method: \"POST\", body: body })\r\n            .then(function (response) {\r\n              // An expired session redirects to /login; don't paste that page in.\r\n              if (!response.ok || response.redirected) { throw new Error(\"preview failed\"); }\r\n              return response.text();\r\n            })\r\n            .then(function (html) { preview.innerHTML = html; })\r\n            .catch(function () {\r\n              preview.textContent = \"Preview unavailable — your session may have expired. \" +\r\n                \"Save your text elsewhere, reload the page, and log in again.\";\r\n            });\r\n        }, 300);\r\n      });\r\n    })();\r\n  </script>\r\n{% endblock Tests still get their own database:\n    the app reads settings.DATABASE_PATH per request, and the autouse fixture\n    above repoints it for every test.\"\"\"\n    import uvicorn\n\n    sock = socket.socket()\n    sock.bind((\"127.0.0.1\", 0))\n    port = sock.getsockname()[1]\n    server = uvicorn.Server(uvicorn.Config(create_app(), log_level=\"warning\"))\n    thread = threading.Thread(target=server.run, kwargs={\"sockets\": [sock]}, daemon=True)\n    thread.start()\n    deadline = time.monotonic() + 10\n    while not server.started:\n        assert time.monotonic() < deadline, \"test server did not start\"\n        time.sleep(0.05)\n    yield f\"http://127.0.0.1:{port}\"\n    server.should_exit = True\n    thread.join(timeout=5)\n    sock.close()\n\n\n@pytest.fixture(scope=\"session\")\ndef browser():\n    sync_api = pytest.importorskip(\"playwright.sync_api\")\n    with sync_api.sync_playwright() as playwright:\n        try:\n            chromium = playwright.chromium.launch()\n        except Exception as exc:  # browser binaries not installed\n            pytest.skip(f\"Chromium unavailable ({exc.__class__.__name__}); \"\n                        \"run `uv run playwright install chromium`\")\n        yield chromium\n        chromium.close()\n\n\n@pytest.fixture\ndef phone(browser, live_server):\n    \"\"\"A fresh 390px-wide browser tab (no cookies) aimed at the test server.\"\"\"\n    context = browser.new_context(viewport=PHONE_VIEWPORT, base_url=live_server)\n    yield context.new_page()\n    context.close()\n\n\ndef log_in(page, role: str) -> None:\n    \"\"\"Log in through the real login form, as a person would.\"\"\"\n    user = DEMO_USERS[role]\n    page.goto(\"/login\")\n    page.fill(\"input[name=email]\", user[\"email\"])\n    page.fill(\"input[name=password]\", user[\"password\"])\n    page.click(\"button[type=submit]\")\n    page.wait_for_url(\"**/admin\")\n\"\"\"T03: Posts — create, edit, delete, test_editor_can_create_a_post_as_draft(client_as):\n    editor = client_as(\"editor\")\n    csrf = _csrf_from(editor)\n    response = editor.post(\n        \"/admin/posts/new\",\n        data={\"title\": \"My First Post\", \"slug\": \"my-first-post\", \"body_md\": \"Hello.\",\n              \"csrf_token\": csrf},\n        follow_redirects=False,\n    )\n    assert response.status_code edit_page.status_code == 200\n    csrf = extract_csrf(edit_page.text)\n    response = editor.post(\n        f\"/admin/posts/{post_id}/edit\",\n        data={\"title\": \"Edited\", \"slug\":    assert updated[\"title\"] == \"Edited\"\n    assert updated[\"body_md\"] == \"v2\"\n\n\ndef test_editor_can_delete_a_post(client_as):\n    editor = client_as(\"editor\")\n    csrf =  <div class=\"page-head\">\r\n    <h1>{{ title }}</h1>\r\n    <a class=\"button button--secondary\" href=\"{{ to_root }}{{ link_prefix }}\">Back to list</a>\r\n  </div>\r\n  {% if error %}<p {"parentUuid":"3ed3e00f-fda6-4d14-ba81-3d91db40251c","isSidechain":false,"promptId":"7d8b3a31-fbfb-487a-80a7-397c044958f9","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01SCPrwAVQttLGYELraDzKXS","type":"tool_result","content":"\"\"\"T09: security and access-control completeness sweep (ticket #10).\n\nNo new feature. Exhaustively re-verifies, across every admin route in the\napp -- not just the one or two routes each earlier ticket happened to test\nas a side effect of its own functional tests:\n\n  1. An anonymous visitor is redirected to /login (never served the page).\n  2. Where a route is admin-only (not editor-accessible), an Editor gets 403.\n  3. Every state-changing (POST) admin form rejects a forged CSRF token.\n\nRoute lists use \"{post_id}\"/\"{page_id}\" placeholders, filled in from real\nrows the `seeded_ids` fixture creates, because some routes (content_crud's\nupdate_item) check the item exists before checking CSRF -- a fake id would\nhit that 303-redirect branch first and never exercise the CSRF check.\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport pytest\n\nfrom app import content as content_module\nfrom app import db as db_module\nfrom tests.conftest import extract_csrf\n\n\n@pytest.fixture\ndef seeded_ids():\n    conn = db_module.connect()\n    try:\n        admin_id = conn.execute(\n            \"SELECT id FROM users WHERE email = 'admin@example.test'\"\n        ).fetchone()[\"id\"]\n        editor_id = conn.execute(\n            \"SELECT id FROM users WHERE email = 'editor@example.test'\"\n        ).fetchone()[\"id\"]\n    finally:\n        conn.close()\n    post_id = content_module.create_content(\"post\", \"Sweep Post\", \"sweep-post\", \"x\", admin_id)\n    page_id = content_module.create_content(\"page\", \"Sweep Page\", \"sweep-page\", \"x\", admin_id)\n    # A real, distinct-from-admin user id for the users.py routes below --\n    # not post_id/page_id, which are content ids, not user ids.\n    return {\"post_id\": post_id, \"page_id\": page_id, \"user_id\": editor_id}\n\n\nGET_ROUTES_EDITOR_OR_ADMIN = [\n    \"/admin\", \"/admin/content\",\n    \"/admin/posts\", \"/admin/posts/new\", \"/admin/posts/{post_id}/edit\",\n    \"/admin/pages\", \"/admin/pages/new\", \"/admin/pages/{page_id}/edit\",\n]\nGET_ROUTES_ADMIN_ONLY = [\"/admin/users\", \"/admin/users/new\"]\nALL_GET_ROUTES = GET_ROUTES_EDITOR_OR_ADMIN + GET_ROUTES_ADMIN_ONLY\n\nPREVIEW_ROUTES = [\"/admin/posts/preview\", \"/admin/pages/preview\"]\n\n# (path template, form body without csrf_token) for every state-changing\n# route gated by require_editor_or_admin.\nPOST_ROUTES_EDITOR_OR_ADMIN = [\n    (\"/admin/posts/new\", {\"title\": \"x\", \"slug\": \"x\", \"body_md\": \"x\"}),\n    (\"/admin/posts/{post_id}/edit\", {\"title\": \"x\", \"slug\": \"x\", \"body_md\": \"x\"}),\n    (\"/admin/posts/{post_id}/delete\", {}),\n    (\"/admin/pages/new\", {\"title\": \"x\", \"slug\": \"x\", \"body_md\": \"x\"}),\n    (\"/admin/pages/{page_id}/edit\", {\"title\": \"x\", \"slug\": \"x\", \"body_md\": \"x\"}),\n    (\"/admin/pages/{page_id}/delete\", {}),\n]\n\n# Same shape, but gated by require_admin specifically.\nPOST_ROUTES_ADMIN_ONLY = [\n    (\"/admin/posts/{post_id}/publish\", {}),\n    (\"/admin/posts/{post_id}/unpublish\", {}),\n    (\"/admin/pages/{page_id}/publish\", {}),\n    (\"/admin/pages/{page_id}/unpublish\", {}),\n    (\"/admin/users/new\", {\"email\": \"sweep-create@example.test\", \"password\": \"x\", \"role\": \"editor\"}),\n    (\"/admin/users/{user_id}/role\", {"parentUuid":"b9007c94-262e-4131-8f94-8b7c13f994bb","isSidechain":false,"promptId":"7d8b3a31-fbfb-487a-80a7-397c044958f9","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01EmEa97Ek9S4WHGuz1T1qkN","type":"tool_result","content":"\"\"\"The FastAPI application.\r\n\r\nThe admin console lives under /admin; the public site is served at the same\r\npaths the static export writes. Routes live in their own modules under\r\napp/routes/ and are included here. Keep this file small.\r\n\"\"\"\r\nfrom __future__ import annotations\r\n\r\nfrom fastapi import FastAPI, Request\r\nfrom fastapi.responses import RedirectResponse\r\n\r\nfrom app.guards import AccessDenied, NeedsLogin, render_access_denied\r\nfrom app.routes import auth, dashboard, pages, posts, public, users\r\n\r\n\r\ndef create_app() -> FastAPI:\r\n    app = FastAPI(title=\"IPHS 400 MP2 CMS\")\r\n    app.include_router(auth.router)\r\n    app.include_router(dashboard.router)\r\n    app.include_router(posts.router)\r\n    app.include_router(pages.router)\r\n    app.include_router(users.router)\r\n    app.include_router(public.router)  # last: its /{slug}.html matches broadly\r\n\r\n    @app.exception_handler(NeedsLogin)\r\n    def _redirect_to_login(request: Request, exc: NeedsLogin):\r\n        return RedirectResponse(\"/login\", status_code=303)\r\n\r\n    app.add_exception_handler(AccessDenied, render_access_denied)\r\n\r\n    return app\r\n\r\n\r\napp = create_app()\r\napp/seed.py:14:    db_module.init_db(db_path)\nBinary file app/__pycache__/db.cpython-313.pyc matches\nBinary file app/__pycache__/seed.cpython-313.pyc matches","is_error":false}]},"uuid":"0326f87d-fc84-469b-831a-3c0f8b681bae","timestamp":"2026-10-07T05:14:51.999Z","toolUseResult":{"stdout":"\"\"\"The FastAPI application.\r\n\r\nThe admin console lives under /admin; the public site is served at the same\r\npaths the static export writes. Routes live in their own modules under\r\napp/routes/ and are included here. Keep this file small.\r\n\"\"\"\r\nfrom __future__ import annotations\r\n\r\nfrom fastapi import FastAPI, Request\r\nfrom fastapi.responses import RedirectResponse\r\n\r\nfrom app.guards import AccessDenied, NeedsLogin, render_access_denied\r\nfrom app.routes import auth, dashboard, pages, posts, public, users\r\n\r\n\r\ndef create_app() -> FastAPI:\r\n    app = FastAPI(title=\"IPHS 400 MP2 CMS\")\r\n    app.include_router(auth.router)\r\n    app.include_router(dashboard.router)\r\n    app.include_router(posts.router)\r\n    app.include_router(pages.router)\r\n    app.include_router(users.router)\r\n    app.include_router(public.router)  # last: its /{slug}.html matches broadly\r\n\r\n    @app.exception_handler(NeedsLogin)\r\n    def _redirect_to_login(request: Request, exc: NeedsLogin):\r\n        return RedirectResponse(\"/login\", status_code=303)\r\n\r\n    app.add_exception_handler(AccessDenied, render_access_denied)\r\n\r\n    return app\r\n\r\n\r\napp = create_app()\r\napp/seed.py:14:    db_module.init_db(db_path)\nBinary file app/__pycache__/db.cpython-313.pyc matches\nBinary file app/__pycache__/seed.cpython-313.pyc matches","stderr":"","interrupted":false,"isImage":false,"noOutputExpected":false},"serverClassifierContext":{"request":"ef2c70ad-b55f-40df-b3b5-c278531f246a","context":{"git_state":{"cwd":"/c/Rajan/E disk/My work/IPHS400/code/mp2-cms","root":"/c/Rajan/E disk/My work/IPHS400/code/mp2-cms","branch":"main","default_branch":null,"status":{"clean":false,"counts":{"staged":0,"modified":1,"untracked":null,"untracked_normal":1},"porcelain":null,"truncated":false},"visibility":{"origin":{"host":"github.com","remote":"Rajan-Thakur/iphs400-mp2-cms","visibility":"public"},"push_remote":"origin","remotes":[{"name":"origin","host":"github.com","remote":"Rajan-Thakur/iphs400-mp2-cms","visibility":"public"},{"name":"template","host":"github.com","remote":"jon-chun/iphs400-mp2-cms-starter","visibility":"unknown"}],"visibility_cache":[{"host":"github.com","remote":"Rajan-Thakur/iphs400-mp2-cms","visibility":"public"}]}},"live_cwd":"/c/Rajan/E disk/My work/IPHS400/code/mp2-cms","platform":"windows"}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"b9007c94-262e-4131-8f94-8b7c13f994bb","session_id":"79460543-7a02-4f77-9f1a-bef5b403f7a1","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\Rajan\\E disk\\My work\\IPHS400\\code\\mp2-cms","sessionId":"79460543-7a02-4f77-9f1a-bef5b403f7a1","version":"2.1.292","gitBranch":"main"}

{"parentUuid":"5e31a13b-376d-4c3f-b0ab-aec3035d9532","isSidechain":false,"promptId":"7d8b3a31-fbfb-487a-80a7-397c044958f9","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01Km7RyX9WrySk32s7RdktfH","type":"tool_result","content":"\"\"\"The `cms` command: serve, publish, deploy.\r\n\r\n    uv run cms serve      # admin console + public preview at http://localhost:8000\r\n    uv run cms publish    # render site/ from published content\r\n    uv run cms deploy     # push site/ to the gh-pages branch (Pages serves it)\r\n\"\"\"\r\nfrom __future__ import annotations\r\n\r\nimport argparse\r\nimport subprocess\r\nimport sys\r\n\r\nfrom app import settings\r\nfrom app.publish import render_site\r\n\r\n\r\ndef main(argv: list[str] | None = None) -> int:\r\n    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(prog=\"cms\")\r\n    sub = parser.add_subparsers(dest=\"command\", required=True)\r\n    serve = sub.add_parser(\"serve\", help=\"run the admin console locally\")\r\n    serve.add_argument(\"--port\", type=int, default=8000)\r\n    serve.add_argument(\"--reload\", action=\"store_true\", default=True)\r\n    sub.add_parser(\"publish\", help=\"render site/ from published content\")\r\n    deploy = sub.add_parser(\"deploy\", help=\"push site/ to gh-pages\")\r\n    deploy.add_argument(\"--message\", default=\"Publish site\")\r\n    args = parser.parse_args(argv)\r\n\r\n    if args.command == \"serve\":\r\n        import uvicorn\r\n\r\n        uvicorn.run(\"app.main:app\", port=args.port, reload=args.reload)\r\n        return 0\r\n\r\n    if args.command == \"publish\":\r\n        out = render_site()\r\n        print(f\"Wrote {out}. Preview it with:  python3 -m http.server -d {out} 8001\")\r\n        return 0\r\n\r\n    if args.command == \"deploy\":\r\n        if not settings.SITE.exists():\r\n            print(\"site/ does not exist yet — run `uv run cms publish` first.\")\r\n            return 1\r\n        result = subprocess.run(\r\n            [sys.executable, \"-m\", \"ghp_import\", \"-n\", \"-p\", \"-m\", args.message,\r\n             str(settings.SITE)],\r\n        )\r\n        if result.returncode == 0:\r\n            print(\"Pushed to gh-pages. Settings -> Pages -> Deploy from a branch -> \"\r\n                  \"gh-pages / root, then wait up to 10 minutes.\")\r\n        return result.returncode\r\n    return 1\r\n\r\n\r\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\r\n    raise SystemExit(main())\r\n#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"Create demo data so a grader (and you) can use the CMS immediately.\n\n    uv run python scripts/seed_demo.py\n\nCreates one admin and one editor user (passwords read from .env, never\nhard-coded). As later tickets add Posts/Pages, this script grows to seed\nexample content too.\n\nThe rubric expects this to run clean on a fresh clone with .env.example values\n(item E4), because the database itself is never committed.\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport os\nimport sys\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nsys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]))\n\nfrom app import settings  # noqa: E402\nfrom app.seed import seed_pages, seed_posts, seed_users  # noqa: E402\n\n\ndef main() -> int:\n    admin_pw = os.environ.get(\"CMS_ADMIN_PASSWORD\")\n    editor_pw = os.environ.get(\"CMS_EDITOR_PASSWORD\")\n    if not admin_pw or not editor_pw:\n        print(\"Set CMS_ADMIN_PASSWORD and CMS_EDITOR_PASSWORD in .env \"\n              \"(copy .env.example).\")\n        return 1\n\n    seed_users(settings.DATABASE_PATH, admin_pw, editor_pw)\n    seed_posts(settings.DATABASE_PATH)\n    seed_pages(settings.DATABASE_PATH)\n    print(f\"Seeded admin/editor users, example posts (one published, one draft), \"\n          f\"and an example page into {settings.DATABASE_PATH}.\")\n    return 0\n\n\n\"\"\"Demo user and content creation, shared by scripts/seed_demo.py and the\ntest fixtures.\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nfrom app import db as db_module\nfrom app.security import hash_password\n\nDEMO_EMAILS = {\"admin\": \"admin@example.test\", \"editor\": \"editor@example.test\"}\n\n\ndef seed_users(db_path: Path, admin_password: str, editor_password: str) -> None:\n    db_module.init_db(db_path)\n    conn = db_module.connect(db_path)\n    try:\n        conn.execute(\n            \"INSERT OR IGNORE INTO users (email, password_hash, role, active) \"\n            \"VALUES (?, ?, 'admin', 1)\",\n            (DEMO_EMAILS[\"admin\"], hash_password(admin_password)),\n        )\n        conn.execute(\n            \"INSERT OR IGNORE INTO users (email, password_hash, role, active) \"\n            \"VALUES (?, ?, 'editor', 1)\",\n            (DEMO_EMAILS[\"editor\"], hash_password(editor_password)),\n        )\n        conn.commit()\n    finally:\n        conn.close()","is_error":false}]},"uuid":"8f555d1c-05ba-44ce-b6de-137cf15ced9b","timestamp":"2026-10-07T05:14:59.980Z","toolUseResult":{"stdout":"\"\"\"The `cms` command: serve, publish, deploy.\r\n\r\n    uv run cms serve      # admin console + public preview at http://localhost:8000\r\n    uv run cms publish    # render site/ from published content\r\n    uv run cms deploy     # push site/ to the gh-pages branch (Pages serves it)\r\n\"\"\"\r\nfrom __future__ import annotations\r\n\r\nimport argparse\r\nimport subprocess\r\nimport sys\r\n\r\nfrom app import settings\r\nfrom app.publish import render_site\r\n\r\n\r\ndef main(argv: list[str] | None = None) -> int:\r\n    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(prog=\"cms\")\r\n    sub = parser.add_subparsers(dest=\"command\", required=True)\r\n    serve = sub.add_parser(\"serve\", help=\"run the admin console locally\")\r\n    serve.add_argument(\"--port\", type=int, default=8000)\r\n    serve.add_argument(\"--reload\", action=\"store_true\", default=True)\r\n    sub.add_parser(\"publish\", help=\"render site/ from published content\")\r\n    deploy = sub.add_parser(\"deploy\", help=\"push site/ to gh-pages\")\r\n    deploy.add_argument(\"--message\", default=\"Publish site\")\r\n    args = parser.parse_args(argv)\r\n\r\n    if args.command == \"serve\":\r\n        import uvicorn\r\n\r\n        uvicorn.run(\"app.main:app\", port=args.port, reload=args.reload)\r\n        return 0\r\n\r\n    if args.command == \"publish\":\r\n        out = render_site()\r\n        print(f\"Wrote {out}. Preview it with:  python3 -m http.server -d {out} 8001\")\r\n        return 0\r\n\r\n    if args.command == \"deploy\":\r\n        if not settings.SITE.exists():\r\n            print(\"site/ does not exist yet — run `uv run cms publish` first.\")\r\n            return 1\r\n        result = subprocess.run(\r\n            [sys.executable, \"-m\", \"ghp_import\", \"-n\", \"-p\", \"-m\", args.message,\r\n             str(settings.SITE)],\r\n        )\r\n        if result.returncode == 0:\r\n            print(\"Pushed to gh-pages. Settings -> Pages -> Deploy from a branch -> \"\r\n                  \"gh-pages / root, then wait up to 10 minutes.\")\r\n        return result.returncode\r\n    return 1\r\n\r\n\r\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\r\n    raise SystemExit(main())\r\n#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"Create demo data so a grader (and you) can use the CMS immediately.\n\n    uv run python scripts/seed_demo.py\n\nCreates one admin and one editor user (passwords read from .env, never\nhard-coded). {"parentUuid":"e1906bdf-8e1c-4d53-a73b-2f8f51ced2c5","isSidechain":false,"message":{"model":"claude-opus-5-5","id":"msg_011CfnJktkTNLSQwLDreAd3N","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"Your three answers fix the design. Before I write any tests, I need you to approve where they go, and two smaller design choices.\n\n## T15 design\n\n- **Storage:** a new `revisions` table, one row per saved version: the item, its title, slug and body, who saved it, when, and whether the save was a rollback.\n- **When a revision is recorded:**\n  - when an item is created\n  - on every edit\n  - on every rollback (as a new row, recorded as \"rolled back to version N\")\n  - not on publish or unpublish\n- **Editor screen:** under the form, a \"Revisions\" list, newest first. Each row shows the version number, who saved it, when, and the title. A collapsible part shows that version's Markdown. The newest row is marked **Current**; every other row gets a **Roll back** button, which is a POST with a CSRF token. Editors and Admins can both use it.\n- **Rollback reuses the editor's save path**, so it gets the same slug checks for free. If the old slug is now taken, you see the editor with \"That slug is already used by another post\", and nothing changes.\n- **Delete:** deleting an item also deletes its revisions.\n\n## Design choice A: how your existing `cms.db` gets the new table\n\nRight now the schema is only created when the seed script runs. So the app would crash on the new table until you re-seed.\n\nMy fix: `db.connect()` brings the database up to date the first time it opens each file. `CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS` is safe to run more than once, so this works on old and new databases alike. It runs once per process.\n\n## Design choice B: items saved before T15\n\nItems in your database from before T15 have no revisions. The first edit after T15 would overwrite them with no way back.\n\nMy fix: that same update step gives each such item one starting revision, using its current text, its author, and its last-updated time. That way their pre-T15 version can be rolled back to.\n\n## Test seams to approve\n\n1. **The admin web pages (the main seam).** I'll drive them through the logged-in test client, the same way `tests/test_posts.py` does, and check results with `content.get_content` plus the edit page itself:\n   - saving creates a revision that shows on the edit page\n   - rollback brings back the old title, slug and body, and adds a \"rolled back\" revision\n   - a slug conflict during rollback shows the error and changes nothing\n   - an Editor can roll back\n   - a revision id that belongs to a *different* item is refused (so nobody can copy one item's text into another)\n2. **Updating an existing database.** Build a pre-T15 database by hand (no `revisions` table, one item already in it), open it, and check that the table now exists and the item has its one starting revision.\n3. **Adding to the existing safety tests** (no new seams): the rollback route goes into the access-control and CSRF sweep, and the edit screen with revisions goes into the phone-width and link tests.\n\nI'll use Posts for the behaviour tests. Pages go through the same `build_content_router`, so one Pages test should be enough to show both are wired up.\n\nIf you're happy with A, B and the seams, I'll create the T15 issue (labelled `stretch`) and start on the first failing test.\n\nTwo things only you can do (both still pending):\n- **Push `d4d5b4a`.** `main` is still one commit ahead of GitHub. Run `! git push`, or tell me to push it.\n- **Set the phase file:** `! echo T15 > .claude/state/phase`"}],"container":null,"stop_reason":"end_turn","stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":2345,"cache_read_input_tokens":71896,"output_tokens":1322,"output_tokens_details":{"thinking_tokens":210},"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":2345,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0},"inference_geo":"not_available","iterations":[{"input_tokens":2,"output_tokens":1322,"cache_read_input_tokens":71896,"cache_creation_input_tokens":2345,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":2345},"type":"message"}],"speed":"standard","fallback_credit":null},"input_transformations":[],"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"serverClassifierRequest":"be37f27e-3478-4cf9-98b4-8c421c522d03","apiBlockIndex":1,"requestId":"req_011CfnJktULXZxvaQmqeadVN","attributionSkill":"mattpocock-skills:tdd","attributionPlugin":"mattpocock-skills","type":"assistant","uuid":"8c407a4f-4deb-4eff-8f58-bb0b2f0bce63","timestamp":"2026-10-07T05:15:13.960Z","advisorModel":"claude-opus-5-5","effort":"high","perTurnEffort":"high","session_id":"79460543-7a02-4f77-9f1a-bef5b403f7a1","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\Rajan\\E disk\\My work\\IPHS400\\code\\mp2-cms","sessionId":"79460543-7a02-4f77-9f1a-bef5b403f7a1","version":"2.1.292","gitBranch":"main"}
